We find that reasoning generally reduces bias, lowering the average bias rate from 0.64 to 0.40, but the effect varies substantially across models. Meanwhile, code quality is not consistently preserved, with the average quality dropping from 0.72 to 0.59. Biased reasoning strongly predicts biased code, and adjusting generation configurations alone is insufficient for robust mitigation. Based on these findings, we propose ProbeDebias, a reasoning-aware framework that detects and rewrites biased reasoning traces before code generation. ProbeDebias achieves 87.76% F1 for reasoning-bias detection and reduces code bias by 83.73% on average while largely preserving quality. Compared with SOTA baselines, it further reduces average bias by 52.70%-54.42% and improves quality by 9.79%-36.79%. These results highlight the value of reasoning-stage analysis for trustworthy code generation.

</details>


### [133] [Statistical Analysis of Executability and Program Equivalence in Decompilation for IoT Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06960)
*Minami Yoda,Jialong Li,Yasuyuki Tahara,Yuichi Sei,Yutaka Matsuno*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对IoT固件反编译质量评估问题，提出一个九维质量评估指标，涵盖结构、行为和语义三个相似度类别。基于OpenWrt平台的318个程序，使用五种方法生成19,625个反编译结果并进行统计分析，发现重编译成功组在各项指标上显著优于失败组（总体Cohen's d=0.92），为量化IoT设备实现缺陷和黑盒生成模型质量评估提供了统计基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备处理敏感隐私信息，反编译是检测其固件漏洞的关键技术。虽然LLM大幅提升了反编译代码的可读性和重编译成功率，但由于其概率性token预测特性，LLM倾向于生成语法正确但语义可能与原始二进制不同的代码，容易丢失错误处理流程和边界检查等关键细节。现有评估指标主要关注测试用例通过情况，无法充分识别内部结构被改变但行为看似有效的代码，缺乏多维度的内部结构量化指标。

Method: 提出一个九维质量评估指标，分为三个类别：结构相似度、行为相似度和语义相似度。以OpenWrt（许多商业路由器底层的开源路由器平台）为目标，选取318个程序，使用五种方法（一种基于规则和四种基于LLM）生成19,625个反编译结果，并进行统计分析。通过比较重编译成功组与失败组的得分差异（使用Cohen's d效应量）来验证指标的有效性。

Result: 重编译成功组在整体得分上显著高于失败组（Cohen's d=0.92）；行为相似度的d=0.96，结构相似度的d=0.69，表明这些指标是反编译质量的重要预测因子。通过统计分析验证了所提指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为量化IoT设备实现缺陷提供了统计评估基础，提出的框架可推广到黑盒生成模型的质量评估中，有助于更全面地检测反编译代码中的语义偏差和漏洞隐患。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) devices handle sensitive privacy-related information such as user audio, video, and authentication data, making it essential to detect vulnerabilities in their firmware. Decompilation, a key detection technique, has recently attracted attention because Large Language Models (LLMs) enable high readability and high recompilation success rates. However, because LLM outputs depend on probabilistic token prediction, they tend to prioritize syntactic correctness and may generate plausible-looking code that is semantically different from the original binary. Vulnerabilities often arise in details that are easily lost in this process, such as error-handling flows and boundary checks. Existing evaluation metrics focus mainly on passing test cases and cannot sufficiently identify code whose internal structure has been altered despite appearing behaviorally valid, so a metric that quantifies the internal structure of decompiled code from multiple perspectives is needed. We propose a nine-dimensional quality evaluation metric consisting of three categories: structural, behavioral, and semantic similarity. Targeting 318 programs from OpenWrt, an open-source router platform underlying many commercial routers, we generated 19,625 decompilation results using five methods (one rule-based and four LLM-based) and analyzed them statistically. The recompilation-success group achieved significantly higher overall scores than the failure group (Cohen's d=0.92); behavioral similarity showed d=0.96 and structural similarity d=0.69, demonstrating that these metrics are important predictors of decompilation quality. This study provides a statistical evaluation foundation for quantifying implementation defects in IoT devices and a framework that generalizes to quality evaluation of black-box generative models.

</details>


### [134] [What Are Developers Actually Discussing When Visual Regression Tests Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07020)
*Miku Watanabe,Kosei Horikawa,Brittany Reid,Yutaro Kashiwa,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析307个使用Chromatic视觉回归测试（VRT）的PR和299个仅含图片的PR，发现VRT-PR虽在验收率上无显著差异，但解决时间更长（3.8倍）、讨论更多（10倍）、代码变更更大（1.75-4.5倍）。通过对189个VRT标记问题的卡片分类，识别出七类缺陷（布局39.7%、外观27.5%、颜色14.8%等），其中约18.5%的问题涉及非风格性根源。研究表明VRT不仅是风格检查器，还是代码变更意外后果的次级检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉回归测试被广泛用于检测UI的意外视觉变化，但学术界对其实际效果、缺陷类型和局限性缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在通过大规模实证分析揭示VRT的真实行为模式及其在维护工具链中的角色。

Method: 收集307个来自103个GitHub仓库的包含Chromatic VRT结果的PR，并与299个含图片但无VRT的PR对比。采用定量分析（验收率、解决时间、讨论数量、代码变更量）和卡片分类法（对189个VRT标记的issue进行分类）相结合的方法。

Result: VRT-PR与Visual PR在验收率上无显著差异，但VRT-PR的解决时间中位数长3.8倍、讨论评论多10倍、代码变更大1.75-4.5倍。VRT结果通常在评审过程中段共享。识别出七类缺陷中前三类为风格性（Layout、Appearance、Color），约18.5%的问题涉及非风格性根源，包括未定义组件状态、内容消失和视觉不可感知的回归。还发现VRT能检测到由无关文件代码变更引发的非局部视觉回归。

Conclusion: VRT除了作为风格检查器的主要角色外，还充当代码变更意外后果的次级检测器。这为VRT应如何集成到维护工具链中提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Visual Regression Tests (VRTs) are widely adopted as a mechanism for detecting unintended visual changes in user interfaces. By design, VRTs operate on rendered pixel output, and the prevailing assumption is that they catch stylistic regressions such as layout shifts, color mismatches, and font alterations. We conduct an empirical analysis of 307 pull requests (PRs) from 103 GitHub repositories that incorporate VRT results via Chromatic, comparing them against 299 PRs that contain image attachments but no VRT (Visual PRs). Quantitatively, VRT-PRs show no significant acceptance-rate difference, but exhibit a 3.8 times longer median resolution time, 10 times more discussion comments, and 1.75 to 4.5 times larger code changes than Visual PRs. VRT results are typically shared around the midpoint of the review process, sustaining ongoing discussion rather than serving only as a final check. Through a card-sorting analysis of 189 VRT-flagged issues, we identify seven defect categories assigned to the analyzed issues: Layout (39.7\%), Appearance (27.5\%), Color (14.8\%), Text (9.5\%), State (6.9\%), Test (6.3\%), and Image (4.2\%). The three most frequent categories are stylistic, while approximately 18.5\% of analyzed issues (35/189) involve non-stylistic origins, including undefined component state (13 cases), content disappearance (17 cases across multiple categories), and visually imperceptible regressions (5 cases). We further document cases in which VRT detected visual regressions originating from code changes in seemingly unrelated files, exposing non-local effects that no targeted test would have been written to catch. These observations indicate that, in addition to its primary role as a stylistic checker, VRT functions as a secondary detector of unintended consequences of code changes, with implications for how VRT should be integrated into the maintenance toolchain.

</details>


### [135] [CAS2UML: A Handwritten Sketch-to-PlantUML Dataset for Class and Activity Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07036)
*Simon Scholz,Mersedeh Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAS2UML数据集，包含557张手工绘制的UML图（271张类图和286张活动图），每张图都配有手工验证的PlantUML代码，并提供基于PlantUML的验证工具和可重用的脚本来检查生成的UML工件的语法正确性和可渲染性，从而实现草图到UML方法的可复现基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型和多模态AI的兴起，从草图和图像自动生成UML重新受到关注。然而，由于缺乏带有可执行groundtruth模型的公开数据集，可复现的评估仍然很困难。

Method: 构建了一个包含557张手工绘制UML图的公共数据集，包括类图和活动图，每张图配对手工验证的PlantUML代码；同时提供基于PlantUML的验证工具和可重用脚本来检查生成UML工件的语法正确性和可渲染性。

Result: 提供了完整的数据集（557张图）、验证工具、处理脚本、文档和演示视频，全部公开可用，支持草图到UML方法的可复现基准测试。

Conclusion: 通过公开数据集和验证工具链，解决了草图到UML评估缺乏可复现性的问题，为相关研究提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Automated UML generation from sketches and images is gaining renewed attention with the rise of large language models and multimodal AI. However, reproducible evaluation remains difficult due to the lack of public datasets with executable groundtruth models. We present CAS2UML, a public dataset of 557 handdrawn UML diagrams, including 271 class diagrams and 286 activity diagrams, each paired with manually validated PlantUML code. We also provide a PlantUML-based validation tool and reusable scripts for checking the syntactic correctness and renderability of generated UML artifacts, enabling reproducible benchmarking of sketch-to-UML approaches. The dataset, validation tool, processing scripts, documentation, and demonstration video are publicly available at: Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Seym0n /cas2uml_hand-drawn_to_plantuml_dataset; Tool and Scripts: https://github.com/Seym0n/handwritten-uml-dataset; Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KQrYeGgT3hs.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Text Matching: Towards Reference-Free Evaluation for Human-Oriented Binary Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07038)
*Xiuwei Shang,Li Hu,Xiao Jiang,Jieke Shi,Junda He,Zhou Yang,Shaoyin Cheng,Guoqiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究LLM-as-a-Judge范式在人类导向二进制逆向工程（HOBRE）评估中的应用，提出了基于专家标注的无参考基准BinJudgeBench，并设计了自适应路由机制BinJudge，在相关性上提升4.5%-24.7%，同时将API成本降低至静态最优配置的0.06-0.84倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HOBRE旨在将反编译伪代码转换为更人类友好的表示。然而其评估面临根本性挑战：人工评估成本高、难以扩展；现有自动指标要么需要可执行测试用例和运行时环境（真实二进制往往不可用），要么依赖通常不可获取的高质量源代码参考，且无法捕捉语义等价但词法多样的输出。虽然LLM-as-a-Judge天然适合HOBRE评估，但其有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出BinJudgeBench——首个基于多维人工判断的专家标注、无需参考的评估基准；系统性研究了三种代表性任务（函数名恢复、二进制代码摘要、反编译优化）上的LLM-as-a-Judge配置；分析了骨干LLM、提示策略和解码温度等配置的影响；提出BinJudge，采用轻量级路由机制为每个任务和样本自适应选择最优judge配置。

Result: LLM-as-a-Judge在BinJudgeBench上达到63.20%的平均人类判断相关性，远超传统自动指标的35.04%。不存在"一刀切"的最优配置，最佳设置因任务和样本而异。BinJudge将相关性提升4.5%-24.7%，API成本降至静态最优配置的0.06-0.84倍。

Conclusion: LLM-as-a-Judge为HOBRE提供了可扩展、高性价比且高保真的自动化评估方案。BinJudge通过自适应路由机制实现了任务级和样本级的最优评估配置选择，为HOBRE领域建立了有效的自动化评估范式。

Abstract: Human-Oriented Binary Reverse Engineering (HOBRE) aims to transform decompiled pseudocode into a more human-friendly representation, thereby reducing the cognitive burden of reverse analysis and improving efficiency. However, reliably evaluating HOBRE outputs remains a fundamental challenge: human evaluation is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale, while existing automated metrics either require executable test cases and runtime environments that are often unavailable for real-world binaries, or rely on high-quality source code references that are typically inaccessible and fail to capture semantically equivalent but lexically diverse outputs. Although LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm is naturally well-suited to HOBRE evaluation, its effectiveness remains underexplored.
  This paper presents the first systematic investigation of the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm for HOBRE across three representative tasks: function name recovery, binary code summarization, and decompilation optimization. We introduce BinJudgeBench, the first expert-annotated, reference-free evaluation benchmark based on multi-dimensional human judgment, where LLM-as-a-Judge achieves an average correlation of 63.20\% with human judgment, outperforming traditional automated metrics at 35.04\%. By analyzing judge configurations across backbone LLMs, prompting strategies, and decoding temperatures, we find that no ``one-size-fits-all'' configuration exists, as the optimal setup varies across tasks and individual samples. To address this, we propose BinJudge, which employs a lightweight routing mechanism to adaptively select the optimal judge configuration for each task and sample. BinJudge improves correlation with human experts by 4.5\%-24.7\% and reduces API cost to 0.06$\times$-0.84$\times$ of that of static best configurations, providing a scalable, cost-effective, and high-fidelity automated evaluation scheme for HOBRE.

</details>


### [137] [Explanation-Guided Metamorphic Testing of Specialized Language Models: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07076)
*Xingcheng Chen,Mehmet Besenk,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，验证了可解释性引导的蜕变测试（explanation-guided metamorphic testing）相比启发式变异策略，能更有效地测试任务特化语言模型的鲁棒性，生成2.30倍的已验证失败触发测试用例，并揭示了模型依赖命名实体和格式线索的捷径行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务特化语言模型已广泛集成到软件工程工作流中（如问题分诊、文档分类、自动化分析），但缺乏关于如何在语义保持的输入变换下测试其鲁棒性和检测脆弱行为的经验证据。现有测试方法缺乏系统性评估框架。

Method: 开展大规模实证研究，涵盖三个数据集、四种模型架构和20种测试配置（由归因方法与变异策略组合而成）。配置结合了基于归因的令牌优先级排序、LLM驱动的变异和自动化语义验证来生成语言学上有效的测试变体，并与启发式基线对比评估失败发现能力、语义有效性和测试效率。

Result: 可解释性引导的蜕变测试比启发式变异策略多生成2.30倍的已验证失败触发测试用例。语义验证显著提升了变异有效性，且在人类标注中，门控接受的变体实现了高标签保持精度。研究还揭示了模型中的系统性捷径行为，包括过度依赖命名实体和格式线索。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可解释性引导的蜕变测试是评估垂直AI应用中任务特化语言模型鲁棒性的有效且实用的方法，为模型行为缺陷的实证检测提供了可靠路径。

Abstract: \head{Background} Task-specialized language models are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows to support vertical-domain activities such as issue triaging, document classification, and automated analysis. Despite their adoption, there is limited empirical evidence on how to test their robustness and detect brittle behaviors under semantics-preserving input transformations.
  \head{Aims} This paper investigates whether explainability-guided metamorphic testing can improve the effectiveness and validity of robustness testing for specialized language models compared to heuristic mutation strategies.
  \head{Method} We conduct a large-scale empirical study of explanation-guided metamorphic testing across three datasets, four model architectures, and 20 testing configurations derived from combinations of attribution methods and mutation strategies. The evaluated configurations combine attribution-based token prioritization, LLM-driven mutation, and automated semantic verification to generate linguistically valid test variants. We assess failure discovery capability, semantic validity, and testing efficiency against heuristic baselines.
  \head{Results} Explanation-guided metamorphic testing generates 2.30$\times$ more verified failure-inducing test cases than heuristic mutation strategies. Semantic verification substantially improves mutation validity and achieves high label-preservation precision among gate-accepted variants according to human annotation. The study further reveals systematic shortcut behaviors across models, including over-reliance on named entities and formatting cues.
  \head{Conclusions} The results provide evidence that explanation-guided metamorphic testing is an effective and practical approach for empirically evaluating the robustness of task-specialized language models used in vertical AI applications.

</details>


### [138] [Rust Coreutils: Rebuilding Unix Foundations in a Modern Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07135)
*Sylvestre Ledru,Samuel Tardieu,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文讲述了用Rust语言重新实现GNU coreutils（Rust coreutils）的开发历程，该项目已成为GNU coreutils的即插即用替代品，兼容大多数Linux发行版。论文通过对比新旧实现，提供关于创建关键软件可靠替代品的见解，并展示现代编程语言特性如何吸引开发者对遗留软件包的关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNU coreutils是UNIX系统的基础软件包，包含约100个核心命令（如ls、cp、cat），运行在数百万台计算机上。然而其C语言代码库可追溯至1990年代初，属于功能完备的遗留软件。作者希望探讨如何有效地重新实现这一关键软件包，以及为什么要这样做。

Method: 通过开发和维护Rust coreutils这一开源项目，用Rust编程语言全面重新实现GNU coreutils的所有核心命令，并持续与原始的GNU coreutils进行功能对比和兼容性验证，最终达到drop-in replacement（即插即用替代品）的标准。

Result: Rust coreutils已成功达到GNU coreutils替代品的地位，兼容大多数Linux发行版。通过与原始版本的对比分析，论文提炼出创建关键软件可靠替代品的经验，并验证了现代编程语言特性对吸引开发者参与遗留软件项目具有积极作用。

Conclusion: 用现代编程语言（如Rust）重新实现关键遗留软件是可行且有益的。这一过程不仅能够提供可靠的功能等价替代品，还能通过现代编程特性为遗留软件注入新的开发活力，吸引更多社区贡献者参与。

Abstract: GNU core utilities (coreutils) is a crucial package in modern UNIX systems. It comprises around 100 fundamental commands---like ls, cp, and cat---which run every day on millions of computers. However, GNU coreutils is also legacy software, with its C codebase dating back to the early 1990s and arguably feature-complete. If one were to consider reimplementing this essential package, how would they do so effectively, and why? This paper recounts the development of Rust coreutils, a contemporary open source reimplementation of GNU coreutils in the Rust programming language, which has reached the status of a drop-in replacement for GNU coreutils, compatible with most Linux distributions. By comparing Rust coreutils with its ancestor, we offer insights into creating a reliable substitute for critical software and highlight how modern programming features can attract development interest in legacy packages.

</details>


### [139] [Towards Assurance Closure in AI-Native Large-Scale Agile Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07317)
*Ricardo Britto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI原生宣言"愿景，主张人在环上的大规模敏捷软件开发中，人类管控意图、风险与例外，而智能体执行更多工程过程。作者认为实现这一目标不仅需要更好的代码生成，更需要"保证闭合"（assurance closure）——即系统能够确立必须为真之事、获取并评判证据、在变更中保持有效性，并用不确定性约束智能体权限。文章识别了六个残余缺口，提出基于共享语义保证层的六项能力架构，并给出四个研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有工作在形式方法、测试、仿真、保证案例、数字孪生和运行时保证方面提供了许多必要机制，但实现AI原生大规模敏捷软件开发的最终目标，需要使保证推理（assurance reasoning）足够机器可操作。论文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，推动从"更好代码生成"向"完整保证闭合"的范式转变。

Method: 论文采用分析性的方法：首先系统梳理了现有保证机制（形式方法、测试、仿真、保证案例、数字孪生、运行时保证），在此基础上识别出六个残余缺口，提出一个高层架构，该架构包含六项对应能力，并构建在共享语义保证层之上；最后提出四个研究问题以指导后续研究。

Result: 识别出六个使保证推理机器可操作化的残余缺口；提出包含六项能力的高层架构，以共享语义保证层为支撑；提出四个研究问题，旨在将该架构转化为可信赖的、人在环上的AI原生研发体系。

Conclusion: 实现AI原生软件开发的承诺，关键在于保证闭合而非仅代码生成能力的提升。通过构建机器可操作的保证推理能力，可以基于不确定性对智能体权限进行有界约束，最终达成人在环上的、可信赖的AI原生研发。

Abstract: The AI-Native Manifesto envisions large-scale agile software development in which humans increasingly govern intent, risk, and exceptions while agents execute more of the engineering process. Realizing that end-state requires more than better code generation: it requires assurance closure, meaning that the system can establish what must be true, determine and obtain appropriate evidence, judge the credibility of that evidence, preserve its validity through change, and use the resulting uncertainty to bound agent authority. Existing work already provides many of the necessary mechanisms across formal methods, testing, simulation, assurance cases, digital twins, and runtime assurance. We identify six residual gaps in making the surrounding assurance reasoning sufficiently machine-operable, propose a high-level architecture with six corresponding capabilities built on a shared semantic assurance layer, and formulate four research questions to turn that architecture into dependable, human-on-the-loop, AI-native R&D.

</details>


### [140] [PACE: Primitive-Aware Code Evolution for Automated Algorithm Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07395)
*Zhuoliang Xie,Ruihao Zheng,Xiang Xu,Genghui Li,Zhengkun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE（Primitive-Aware Code Evolution），一种将LLM驱动的自动化算法设计从"整体程序演化"解耦为"可执行算法原语（EAP）演化"的方法。PACE通过保持动态EAP集合，实现算法组件的代码级跨程序迁移，并利用基于父代相对性能改进的Thompson采样来指导原语选择，无需额外评估数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化算法设计通常将算法视为完整的、不可分割的程序进行演化。这种"整体程序"视角虽然简化了搜索空间，但将有用的局部逻辑与其宿主程序强耦合——当整个程序被丢弃时，有价值的代码片段也随之消失，导致难以评估单个算法组件的贡献。

Method: PACE的核心方法包括：(1) 将局部逻辑表示为持久化的可执行算法原语（EAPs），与完整程序解耦；(2) 维护一个动态EAP集合以实现代码级跨程序迁移；(3) 采用原语感知的演化算子，从结构上保证算法组件的保留和跨程序转移；(4) 基于父代相对性能改进的Thompson采样来指导EAP选择，无需额外评估数据集。

Result: 在四个任务上的实验表明，PACE能够有效发现具有竞争力的算法，同时从结构上保留有价值的算法组件。

Conclusion: 通过将算法演化从整体程序层面解耦到原语层面，PACE成功解决了代码片段随宿主程序丢失的问题，使有价值的算法逻辑得以保留和复用，为LLM驱动的自动化算法设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based automated algorithm design typically evolves algorithms as complete, indivisible programs. While this whole-program perspective simplifies the search space, it fundamentally couples the useful local logic to its host program. Consequently, valuable code snippets vanish when the overall program is discarded, making it highly difficult to assess the contribution of individual algorithmic components.To address this, we propose Primitive-Aware Code Evolution (PACE), which decouples local logic from complete programs by representing it as persistent units called Executable Algorithmic Primitives (EAPs). To enable code-level transfer, PACE maintains a dynamic set of EAPs. Algorithm evolution is driven by primitive-aware operators that structurally guarantee the retention and cross-program transfer of these components. To evaluate them effectively, PACE leverages Thompson sampling based on parent-relative performance improvements, guiding primitive selection from the set without requiring extra evaluation datasets. Experiments on four tasks demonstrate that PACE effectively discovers competitive algorithms while structurally preserving valuable algorithmic components.

</details>


### [141] [Circuit-Based Program Verification: Sequential Circuits as an Intermediate Representation for Verifying C Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07397)
*Po-Chun Chien,Nian-Ze Lee,Armin Biere,Dirk Beyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Circuit-Based Program Verification (CPV)框架，将C程序翻译为时序电路，利用现成的硬件模型检验器作为后端进行软件验证。该框架支持可达性安全和终止性分析，集成多种硬件模型检验算法（BMC、k-归纳、IC3/PDR），并在16000多个任务上验证了其与五个主流软件验证器的竞争力，且能解决其他验证器无法处理的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件验证和硬件验证虽然都涉及状态转换系统的推理，但两个社区各自发展了独立的中间表示和验证算法，缺乏跨领域的融合。传统软件验证器通常依赖基于路径的探索，难以从整体上分析程序的转移关系。

Method: CPV是一个模块化框架，将C程序翻译为时序电路，将程序的控制流和数据流折叠成单一的整体转移关系，并利用现成的硬件模型检验器作为后端，支持可达性安全和终止性分析，集成了BMC、k-归纳、IC3/PDR等多种验证算法。反例会自动翻译回软件验证证据（witnesses）供用户解读。

Result: 在超过16000个任务的基准套件上进行了全面评估，CPV与五个成熟的软件验证器相比表现出有竞争力的性能，并展现出互补优势，能独特地解决其他验证器无法处理的任务。

Conclusion: 时序电路可以作为软件验证的有效中间表示，使硬件模型检验技术能够直接应用于软件验证领域，实现跨领域验证技术融合，并具有与其他软件验证器互补的能力。

Abstract: Formal verification of software programs and hardware designs shares the common goal of reasoning about state-transition systems, yet the two communities have largely developed separate intermediate representations and verification algorithms. This paper investigates sequential circuits as an intermediate representation for software verification, with the goal of enabling direct application of hardware-model-checking techniques. We present Circuit-Based Program Verification (CPV), a modular framework that translates C programs into sequential circuits and employs off-the-shelf hardware model checkers as backends. Unlike traditional software verifiers, which typically rely on path-based exploration, CPV reasons over sequential circuits, where a program's control and data flows are folded into a monolithic transition relation that can be analyzed as a whole. The framework supports reachability-safety and termination analyses and integrates multiple state-of-the-art hardware model checkers, which together provide access to diverse verification algorithms, including bounded model checking, $k$-induction, and IC3/PDR. Counterexamples found by hardware model checkers are automatically translated back into software-verification witnesses for users to interpret verification results. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation on a benchmark suite of more than 16000 tasks. Our results show that CPV achieved competitive performance against five well-established software verifiers and exhibited complementary strengths by uniquely solving tasks that other verifiers cannot handle.

</details>


### [142] [Taxonomy-Driven Analysis of Open-Source AI Risk Mitigation Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07446)
*Afreen Alam,Evgenija Popchanovska,Ana Gjorgjevikj,Maryan Rizinski,Lubomir T. Chitkushev,Irena Vodenska,Dimitar Trajanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于分类法的结构化协议，通过LLM辅助的RAG管道将21个开源LLM评估与安全工具的能力映射到扩展MIT AI风险缓解与响应分类法的32个子类别中，揭示了工具集中在技术与运营控制、而治理与法规控制严重缺失的不均衡现状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业大规模采用LLM带来了运营、安全和治理风险，但现有工具的生态碎片化，且工具描述使用技术术语，与企业治理框架和风险分类法不对齐，导致难以确定哪些工具解决哪些风险以及关键缺口在哪里。

Method: 提出分类法驱动的AI风险缓解自动化协议：构建RAG管道分析21个开源工具的源代码和文档，提取每个分类法类别对应的能力；采用Fleiss' Kappa评估可靠性，并通过多数投票提升F1分数。

Result: 三位独立评审者间达到中等一致性（Fleiss' Kappa = 0.509）；多数投票后映射协议的F1分数为75.5%；工具能力高度集中于技术与运营控制，而治理、法律与法规、金融与市场控制基本未被覆盖。

Conclusion: 研究提供了企业AI风险类别与开源缓解能力之间的实用映射，识别了仍需要人工监督的领域，并提出了适用于开源和专有解决方案的分类法驱动框架，同时倡导分层风险缓解架构（工具控制+组织与法规流程）。

Abstract: Rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) in enterprise settings has introduced operational, security, and governance risks. As generative AI applications move from pilot to production, manual harm identification and mitigation are becoming difficult to scale. Although many tools support model evaluation, adversarial testing, runtime guardrails, and observability, the tooling landscape remains fragmented. Tools are typically designed for specific engineering tasks and described in technical terms that do not align with governance frameworks or risk taxonomies, making it difficult to determine which tools address which risks and where critical gaps remain. This paper proposes a structured protocol to automate AI risk mitigation through a taxonomy-driven analysis of open-source LLM evaluation and security tools. We map the capabilities of 21 prominent open-source tools to the 32 subcategories of the extended MIT AI Risk Mitigation and Response Taxonomy. An LLM-assisted retrieval-augmented generation pipeline analyzes source code and documentation to extract capabilities for each taxonomy category. Reliability assessment yielded moderate agreement (Fleiss' Kappa = 0.509) among three independent reviewers. The analysis reveals a highly skewed landscape in which tools cluster around technical and operational controls, while governance, legal and regulatory, and financial and market controls remain largely unaddressed. This motivates a layered risk-mitigation architecture combining tool-based controls with organizational and regulatory processes. The mapping protocol achieved an F1 score of 75.5% after majority voting. Overall, the study provides a practical mapping between enterprise AI risk categories and open-source mitigation capabilities, identifies where human oversight remains necessary, and presents a taxonomy-driven framework applicable to open-source and proprietary solutions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MSB-GFM，一个用于跨域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架。它通过多语义基表示学习范式将每个多标签节点建模为语义基的自适应组合，并采用语义-结构双通道架构配合领域对抗训练实现跨域知识迁移，解决了现有图基础模型基于单标签假设导致多标签节点语义纠缠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型（GFMs）建立在单标签假设之上，将节点嵌入为表示空间中的单一向量，这无法准确刻画含有多重语义的多标签节点，导致语义纠缠。同时，现有的多标签节点分类方法仅考虑同领域场景，跨域泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出MSB-GFM框架：1）引入多语义基表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基的自适应组合，增强表征灵活性；2）设计语义-结构双通道架构，并结合领域对抗训练实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验证明了该模型在跨域多标签节点分类任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基表示学习解决了多标签节点的语义纠缠问题，并通过双通道架构和领域对抗训练实现了跨域泛化，为图基础模型在多标签场景下的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [144] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE是一种专为动态字节补丁设计的混合专家（MoE）架构。它用Top-K专家层替代全局补丁Transformer中的密集前馈模块，以补丁熵作为路由坐标，结合补丁长度来管理专家专业化，实现稀疏条件计算。实验表明其在各类基线中取得最低的bits-per-byte，同时保持可比的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级大语言模型虽然通过动态分块实现了免分词器建模，但所有补丁仍使用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据补丁的语义和粒度差异自适应调整模型容量，导致计算资源分配不合理。

Method: 1) 用Top-K专家层替换全局补丁Transformer中的密集前馈模块；2) 每个动态补丁作为专家路由的基本单元，以其字节覆盖范围作为工作负载计量的贡献；3) 路由器直接从补丁熵选择专家，利用与动态补丁构建相同的粒度信号组织稀疏计算；4) 补丁熵和长度联合定义特征空间用于调节专家专业化。

Result: 在匹配的密集和稀疏基线中，EntropyMoE取得了最低的held-out bits-per-byte，同时保持可比的downstream准确率，验证了补丁熵作为稀疏条件计算路由坐标的有效性。

Conclusion: 补丁熵可以作为一种有效的路由坐标用于稀疏条件计算，该工作将混合专家建模扩展到了基于分词器表示之外的领域，为字节级模型的自适应容量分配提供了新途径。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [145] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，用于解释稀疏混合专家（MoE）奖励模型。针对现有基于路由权重解释方法的不足——只能说明专家接收哪些提示，无法说明专家如何评判响应，CoCo通过选取贡献差异最大的chosen-rejected响应对来联合刻画专家的路由和偏好行为。实验表明，CoCo在解释的连贯性、忠实性和专业性上均优于基于路由、基于评分和基于稀疏自编码器的方法，同时保持具有竞争力的奖励建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型对从人类偏好中学习至关重要，但理解其预测依据仍具挑战性。现有的MoE奖励模型可解释性方法仅依靠路由权重来判断专家行为，但路由权重只揭示专家"接收"哪些提示，无法反映专家如何"评判"响应，对专家行为只提供了部分解释，因此需要更全面的解释方法。

Method: 提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）响应级解释方法。核心思想是利用chosen-rejected响应对中贡献差异最大的样本，同时捕捉专家的路由行为（接收什么）和偏好行为（如何评判），从而给出更完整、更忠实的专家角色刻画。通过自动评估和人工评估，与基于路由、评分及稀疏自编码器的方法进行对比。

Result: 在自动和人工评估中，CoCo生成的解释比基于路由、基于评分和基于稀疏自编码器的替代方法在连贯性、忠实性和专业性方面表现更优，同时保持了具有竞争力的奖励建模准确性。

Conclusion: 这是首个对MoE奖励模型解释方法进行系统研究的工作，证明了CoCo作为更全面的专家行为解释工具的有效性，为MoE奖励模型的可解释性研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [146] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的、可解释的、无需训练的无监督社区检测方法，借鉴自然界相变动力学思想，通过初始化、合并、细化、选择四个阶段，利用LLM归纳显式逻辑规则，在真实数据集上达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于目标函数的社区检测方法难以处理复杂图结构，而深度学习方法虽然性能提升但牺牲了可解释性，并且依赖标注数据和训练过程。LLM具备强大的推理能力和世界知识，有望实现可解释、无标签的社区检测，但目前尚缺乏相关方法。

Method: LUCID采用四阶段流水线，受自然系统相变动力学启发：（1）局部视角社区初始化：使用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；（2）多因素社区合并：利用LLM归纳的规则迭代合并局部社区；（3）多粒度社区细化：并行应用LLM归纳的由粗到细规则以减少边界噪声；（4）全局视角社区选择：基于拓扑紧密度和边界清晰度筛选高质量社区。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID作为一种无监督方法达到了最先进的性能，并持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID证明了通过LLM将隐式知识转化为显式可解释的逻辑规则，可以在不依赖标注数据和训练的情况下实现高质量的社区检测，兼顾了性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [147] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ADIAS框架，将自动化智能体设计从以候选智能体为中心转变为以问题为中心（issue-centric），通过持久化问题状态显式携带修复进展，指导后续优化。在五个交互式基准上平均超过最强基线25.2%，消融实验显示移除持久化问题状态或恢复候选中心策略导致性能下降最高40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化智能体设计方法以候选智能体为中心组织跨轮次经验，修复进展是隐式的，导致修复目标定位低效、部分进展整合缓慢、无效干预在轮次间传播。作者希望将修复进展显式化为持久化的问题状态以更有效地指导优化。

Method: 提出问题中心（issue-centric）的智能体优化公式，并实例化为ADIAS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 持久化问题状态（persistent issue state），维护稳定的问题身份、生命周期状态、支持证据和干预-结果历史；(2) 问题引导优化（issue-guided optimization），利用该状态联合提出修复目标和修订方向，进行后续聚焦的全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准上，ADIAS平均超过最强基线25.2%，并在四种骨干模型上取得一致提升。消融实验表明，移除持久化问题状态或用候选中心策略替换问题中心修订，性能下降最高达40.7%。

Conclusion: 问题中心的智能体优化范式明显优于传统的候选中心方法，通过显式持久化问题状态可以有效提升修复目标定位精度、加速部分进展整合，并避免无效干预在轮次间的传播。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [148] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAP方法，通过问题对比教师选择（QCTS）识别样本特定的中间层，并将其注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，从而在第一个语言模型层之前预测视觉token重要性并完成剪枝，无需实际注意力图，在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上以5.56%的视觉token保留97.5%性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）性能强大但受限于大量视觉token的处理成本。视觉token剪枝可降低成本，但需准确估计token重要性。现有方法使用固定中间层的文本到视觉注意力指导剪枝，存在两个问题：(1) 对问题响应最敏感的层随样本变化，固定层次优；(2) 获取合适中间层注意力需付出大量计算，此时已消耗大量算力。

Method: 提出Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP)，包含两个核心组件：(1) Question Contrastive Teacher Selection (QCTS)：通过对比原始问题和参考问题下的注意力，为每个样本选择特定的教师层；(2) 将所选层的注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，该预测器从多模态输入特征估计视觉token重要性。推理时，MAP结合预测的重要性分数与多样性准则，在第一个语言模型层之前剪枝视觉token，无需注意力图。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B的十个基准上，MAP仅保留5.56%的视觉token即可保留未剪枝模型97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速，且与现有推理加速技术兼容。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本自适应的教师层选择和轻量级注意力预测，解决了固定层注意力不可靠和注意力获取计算开销大的双重问题，实现了高效且有效的视觉token剪枝。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [149] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WebGrader，一种自进化的程序化奖励生成器，用于RL训练LLM生成网站。它自动从网站请求中推导交互流程（Flow Contracts），在实时浏览器中执行并收集多维度证据，通过残差驱动的离线循环发现并筛选可复用的验证技能，从而解决奖励设计中手工脚本成本高、VLM评判可能过早判定（未观察到关键状态）的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成完整网站时，RL训练受限于奖励设计。手写浏览器脚本对开放式需求成本高，而VLM和GUI-agent评分器可扩展但可能在观察到决定性状态之前就下发判定，导致奖励信号不准确。

Method: WebGrader包含四个关键部分：（1）从每个网站请求自动推导所需的交互流程，表示为可执行的Flow Contracts；（2）在实时浏览器中物化生成项目，将目标动作锚定到源代码和实时DOM；（3）沿同一浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久化状态证据；（4）通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证器技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，并在策略训练前冻结提升的技能图。将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集和语义判断分离，确保仅在观察到请求的状态转换后才给出Pass判定。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练的8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率，比匹配的外观加脚本奖励高出7.88个百分点，并超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953的满分，超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: WebGrader通过自进化的程序化奖励设计，有效解决了LLM网页生成的RL训练中奖励瓶颈问题，显著提升了功能成功率和模型性能。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [150] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4框架，一种基于成语的跨概念创造力评估方法，包含编码（将目标映射到可意象替代概念）和解码（模型推断含义）两个环节，构建了包含184个合成项和37个人工创建项的C4-Eval数据集，在两种任务设置下测试了10个MLLM，发现最强闭源模型准确率仅约50%，开源模型差距更大，揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在跨概念创造力解码方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造性能力在诸多领域至关重要，但现有的评估侧重于准确性任务，缺乏对创造性尤其是接受性创造力的系统评估。跨概念理解是接受性创造力的核心认知能力，但目前缺乏结构化的评估基准，难以量化和比较模型的这种能力。

Method: 提出C4评估框架：1）构建人工标注并第三方评审的跨概念网络，通过桥接路径将目标槽位映射到可意象的替代概念，实现带显式结构、可批量生成且有精确答案的编码；2）创建C4-Eval测试集，包含184个合成项和37个人工收集的跨概念成语图形，每个项目在五种任务设置中实例化，共884个主要答案恢复案例；3）在10个MLLM上进行评估，测试候选约束、桥接提示和解释请求等不同条件的影响。

Result: 最强闭源模型的主要准确率分别达到50.7%和48.0%，开源模型表现显著较低。候选约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥接提示和解释请求仅带来适度提升。结果表明当前MLLM在通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码含义方面存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在跨概念创造力解码方面存在显著不足，C4框架为系统性评估和推动MLLM创造性推理能力提供了新的基准工具。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [151] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowPlan是一个两阶段的学位规划系统：CatalogBrowse负责从异构的大学官方来源（目录、PDF、JSON、部门页面）中重建课程体系，DegreeMap基于前者输出的结构化文档进行学生个性化的课程路径优化。系统强调提取与规划之间的严格边界，通过可验证的中间契约而非端到端耦合来保证两个阶段的可信度。在100所大学的广泛测试和6所学校的密集测试中，达到了96.2%的库存召回率和100%的硬可行性，整体流水线可认证99.5%的请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学学位规划面临两个按序排列的挑战：首先需要从无统一模式的异构官方来源（目录、部门页面、JSON端点、PDF）重建课程体系，然后才能基于先修逻辑和重叠需求约束优化学生个性化路径。将两者耦合会导致每个失败模式相互掩盖——因为由规划器驱动自身爬取的系统永远学不到当前计划不需要的事实。因此需要一种强制提取优先边界的方法。

Method: KnowPlan强制实施提取优先的边界，并衡量两个阶段之间的接口而非假设其正确性。CatalogBrowse阶段：在无任何用户档案的情况下探索，通过较低置信度的期望边际增益评分合法动作，使用平台适配器和跨度约束的子句到AST模型进行确定性解析，以闭包证书而非奖励阈值终止。输出三个带溯源链接的JSON文档。DegreeMap阶段：仅消费这些文档，将其编译为带类型的需求超图，使用CP-SAT进行字典序优化（硬可行性→完成时限→负载与风险→个性化效用→期权价值），保证在每个阶段在前一阶段已证明的最优解内优化。

Result: 在100所大学的广泛轨道和6所学校的密集轨道上：CatalogBrowse达到96.2%的库存召回率和88.7%的掩码源恢复率，比穷举爬虫节省47%的源访问；DegreeMap保持100.0%的硬可行性，个性化效用比最强基线提升+0.066；完整流水线可认证99.5%的请求，与特权黄金图的效用差距仅为0.015。

Conclusion: KnowPlan证明了在学位规划中，严格分离信息提取与路径优化两个阶段，并以可验证的中间契约连接它们，能够有效避免耦合导致的失败模式互相掩盖问题。这种架构不仅实现了高召回率和高可行性，还保证了规划结果在求解器预算内的可认证性，为多源异构信息驱动的规划任务提供了可靠的范式。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [152] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskSense是一个任务中心的world modeling框架，通过可微分的随机空间注意力机制（由前一个隐状态条件化）在潜在编码前强制执行任务相关性，并辅以逆向动力学目标来引导注意力聚焦于控制相关区域。与完整重建观测不同，该模型仅重建被注意的区域，从而丢弃无关视觉内容，在Distracting Control Suite上显著优于DreamerV3基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统world models通过重建完整观测来学习紧凑的潜在状态，会隐式地促使表征保留整个视觉输入的信息。然而任务相关内容往往只占观测的一小部分，背景杂乱和干扰物会消耗宝贵的表征容量，导致潜在表征偏重建模与任务无关的视觉内容，稀释控制相关特征的学习信号，在视觉干扰下严重损害下游性能。

Method: TaskSense框架：(1) 在潜在编码前使用可微分的随机空间注意力机制（由前一潜在状态条件化）强制执行任务相关性；(2) 通过辅助逆向动力学目标引导注意力聚焦于控制相关区域；(3) 仅重建被注意的区域而非完整观测；(4) 解码器以采样到的注意力图为条件，确保在随机注意力下仍能进行一致的重建。

Result: 与DreamerV3基线相比，TaskSense在DeepMind Control Suite上保持竞争性能，同时在Distracting Control Suite上持续优于DreamerV3，展现出显著提升的视觉干扰鲁棒性。定性分析确认，由逆向动力学监督引导的注意力能持续定位控制相关区域并抑制无关视觉内容。

Conclusion: TaskSense通过任务中心的注意力机制和选择性重建策略，有效解决了视觉重建目标与控制目标之间的不匹配问题，在保持标准任务性能的同时大幅提升了视觉干扰下的鲁棒性，验证了任务相关性先验在world modeling中的重要性。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content. The decoder is further conditioned on the sampled attention map, enabling consistent reconstruction despite stochastic attention. Compared with the DreamerV3 baseline, TaskSense maintains competitive performance on the DeepMind Control Suite while consistently outperforming DreamerV3 on the Distracting Control Suite, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to visual distractions. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the learned attention, guided by inverse-dynamics supervision, consistently localizes control-relevant regions while suppressing irrelevant visual content.

</details>


### [153] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对六个前沿模型在明确引导压力下的行为可引导性进行了系统评估，使用300对基线和引导项目，涵盖价值观冲突、推理诱发和推理抑制三类任务，共产生24,480个判断。研究发现模型不仅在引导幅度上有差异，更在响应模式（mode）上存在本质不同，某些模式仅出现在个别模型中。通过线性探针和干预注入实验，研究在Llama模型中定位了行为差异的内部神经基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管前沿语言模型使用不同的数据、目标和安全流程训练，但这些差异是否会在明确的引导压力下产生可测量的不同行为，此前尚未得到充分探索。理解模型之间的行为可引导性差异对于模型评估、安全对齐和部署决策具有重要意义。

Method: 使用300对基线和引导项目（分属价值观冲突、推理诱发、推理抑制三类，外加40个验证项目），六个模型作为盲审同行裁判，基于固定行为评分标准对每个响应进行分类，产生24,480个判断并通过留一法共识评分。对Llama模型进行内部机制分析，使用线性探针从残差流解码行为（保留准确率0.87），并通过在生成过程中注入该方向进行干预实验（行为从0%驱动到86%）。

Result: 模型不仅在引导改变行为的程度上不同，而且在其产生的响应类型（模式）上存在差异，某些响应模式仅出现在一个或两个模型中。GPT-5在拒绝披露推理过程的同时保持答案完整（99%对比其他模型0%）；Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。在Llama中，线性探针以0.87的保留准确率从残差流解码行为，干预注入将行为从0%驱动到86%，所有发现在token预算补救和假设盲判控制实验条件下均成立。

Conclusion: 前沿模型在引导压力下展现出实质性的、可区分的个体差异，这些差异不仅体现在行为改变幅度上，更体现在响应模式上。模型间的这种差异可以追溯到具体的内部机制（如Llama中的残差流方向），表明不同训练策略产生了可辨识的行为特征，这对理解模型安全性和行为可控性有重要启示。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


### [154] [Automated item evaluation: Predicting item acceptance and rejection using LLM-generated critiques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06609)
*Hotaka Maeda,Yikai Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于文本的自动化试题评估（AIE）模型，利用历史拒绝数据预测试题的接受/拒绝状态。融合DeBERTaV3-large对原始文本和Qwen3生成试题评论的表示，模型在52,759个ELA和数学试题上达到Accuracy=.75、F1=.64、AUC=.80的性能。数学预测效果（F1=.73, AUC=.86）显著优于ELA（F1=.51, AUC=.72）。模型在偏差、敏感性、公平性相关试题的识别上表现不佳，强调了人工审查在这些领域的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统试题评估依赖人工专家审查和现场测试，成本高、效率低。构建一个无需人工审查即可预测试题质量的自动化评估模型，可以显著减轻命题和审题的工作负担，特别是在自动化试题生成日益普及的背景下。

Method: 使用大规模标准化测试项目的52,759个历史试题数据（其中34%被永久拒绝），采用三种模型：1）在原始试题文本上微调DeBERTaV3-large分类器；2）在Qwen3生成的试题评论上训练DeBERTa分类器；3）融合前两者表示的融合模型。同时探索了决策阈值的调整以平衡敏感性和特异性。

Result: 融合模型表现最优（Accuracy=.75, F1=.64, AUC=.80）。数学预测（F1=.73, AUC=.86）明显优于ELA（F1=.51, AUC=.72）。将阈值从.5降至.25可将敏感性提升至.88（ELA）和.91（数学），但特异性降至.31和.56。融合文本评论改善了大多数拒绝原因的预测，但模型在识别偏差、敏感性、公平性和可及性相关试题（尤其ELA）方面表现困难。

Conclusion: 基于文本的AIE在部分领域可行，可作为减少人工审查和现场测试负担的实用工具。然而，对涉及公平性的试题，模型预测能力不足，必须保留人工审查。此外，较难试题被赋予更高的拒绝概率，提示模型可能捕捉到了难度与质量之间的关联。

Abstract: Automated item evaluation (AIE) refers to the use of computational methods to assess item quality without requiring manual expert review or field testing of the items under evaluation. We aimed to build a near-comprehensive AIE model by predicting item acceptance and rejection from item text using historical rejection data from a large-scale standardized testing program. The dataset contained 52,759 English language arts (ELA) and mathematics items with 34% permanently rejected from future operational use. Rejection reasons included poor psychometric properties, content issues, bias and sensitivity concerns, and non-content issues. We fine-tuned a DeBERTaV3-large classifier on raw item text, a second DeBERTa classifier on Qwen3-generated item critiques, and a fusion model combining representations from both. The fusion model achieved the strongest overall performance (Accuracy = .75, F1 = .64, AUC = .80, Sensitivity = .64, Specificity = .81). Prediction for math (F1 = .73, AUC = .86) was considerably more accurate than ELA (F1 = .51, AUC = .72). Lowering the decision threshold from .5 to .25 raised average sensitivity for ELA and math to .88 and .91, while reducing specificity to .31 and .56, respectively, which may be preferable in automated item generation contexts where generating items is cheaper than evaluating them. Incorporating item critiques alongside raw item text improved performance across most rejection reasons. The model assigned higher rejection probabilities to more difficult items. However, the fusion model struggled to identify items flagged for bias, sensitivity, fairness, or accessibility, especially for ELA. These findings suggest that text-based AIE is feasible in some areas and may offer a practical tool for reducing the burden of manual review and field testing, while also underscoring the importance of human review for items with fairness concerns.

</details>


### [155] [NxN E-valuation: Hypothesis Certification via a Conformal CRT Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06621)
*Bin Wang,Yan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NxN E-valuation，一种基于e-value的假设认证算法，利用已有的大规模数据集让样本之间互为零假设，无需构建专门的认证流程即可验证假设，特别适用于LLM探索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在假设生成方面表现优异但存在严重幻觉问题，直接使用LLM输出不可靠。现有解决方案（如循环验证、预留数据测试等）各有缺陷：循环验证存在逻辑自证问题，预留数据测试可能被虚假相关性欺骗。

Method: NxN E-valuation利用自然存在的大规模训练集，让不同样本互为对方的零假设，直接实现条件随机化检验（CRT）来认证每个假设，从而无需为每个具体案例构建专门的认证程序。

Result: 该方法在LLM生成的假设适用于每个独立样本的条件下，可以普遍性地替代LLM循环验证和预留数据测试方法。

Conclusion: 只要数据集足够大，NxN E-valuation就能提供比现有方法更可靠的假设认证机制，为解决LLM幻觉问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose NxN E-valuation, a handy, e-value-based hypothesis-certification algorithm that lets a hypothesis be verified without building any case-specific certification procedure---such as constructing a dedicated null hypothesis---as long as a large enough dataset is available. The method is especially suited to LLM-based exploration systems, where LLMs are remarkably good at proposing hypotheses but suffer badly from hallucination; this hallucination prevents us from harvesting LLM outputs directly, and existing remedies each fall short. The most common solutions include letting the LLM verify or correct itself circular verification and held-out testing (where false hypotheses can still pass via spurious correlations), among other remedies detailed in the introduction. To resolve this, NxN E-valuation exploits the naturally existing large training set and lets different samples serve as null hypotheses for one another. This design directly realizes a conditional randomization test (CRT) that certifies each hypothesis. The approach can be a universally better replacement for at least LLM circular verification and held-out-data testing, provided the LLM's generations are hypotheses that apply to each individual sample.

</details>


### [156] [Shape Your Feed: An LLM-based Agentic System for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06632)
*Ziyun Xu,Bosen Ding,Yue Zhang,Ji Qi,Qingyuan Song,Jizhou Huang,Liwei Wang,Jefferey Santelli,Yue Weng,Qichao Que,Zhenheng Yang,Junfeng Pan,Linhong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Shape Your Feed (SYF)，一个基于LLM的智能体推荐框架，通过三层架构（感知流、服务流、自进化流）实现实时多模态内容协同策展，让用户通过文本、语音和UI交互主动塑造推荐内容，突破了传统被动推荐范式的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业推荐系统采用被动排序范式，仅从隐式行为信号（点击、停留时间）推断用户偏好，无法理解用户的显式自然语言输入，导致用户表达的兴趣与实际推荐内容之间存在持续偏差，用户难以实时表达细腻偏好或操控自己的信息流。

Method: SYF采用三层架构：(i)感知流：从文本提示、语音命令和UI交互中捕获细粒度用户意图；(ii)服务流：基于持久化的语义画像进行实时智能体重排序和候选项目剪枝；(iii)自进化流：通过Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)和LLM-as-a-Judge集成将系统行为与人类判断对齐。

Result: 离线评估显示SYF的对齐评分模块达到98.85%准确率，显著优于强少样本基线；大规模线上A/B实验表明SYF提升了信息流相关性和用户情感指标，验证了其在工业场景中的实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: SYF证明了交互式、用户可操控的推荐系统在工业环境中是可行且可扩展的，为推荐系统从被动预测向主动协同策展的范式转变提供了实际路径。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems predominantly adopt a passive ranking paradigm that infers user preferences from implicit behavioral signals (e.g., clicks, dwell time) rather than explicit, natural language inputs. As a result, users experience a persistent discrepancy between their explicit interests and what passive behavioral algorithms deliver, limiting their ability to express nuanced preferences or steer their feed in real time. To address this growing gap between how recommendations are optimized and how users wish to articulate their interests, we present Shape Your Feed (SYF), an LLM-based agentic recommendation framework that enables real-time, multimodal co-curation of content. SYF employs a three-tier architecture: (i) a Perception Flow that captures fine-grained user intent from text prompts, voice commands, and UI interactions; (ii) a Serving Flow that performs real-time agentic re-ranking and pruning of candidate items, grounded in a persistent Semantic Profile encoding evolving user preferences; and (iii) a Self-Evolution Flow that aligns system behavior with human judgments via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and an LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble. Offline evaluations show that SYF's alignment scoring module achieves 98.85% accuracy, substantially improving over strong few-shot baselines. Large-scale online A/B experiments on production traffic further demonstrate that SYF improves feed relevance and user sentiment, indicating a practical and scalable path toward interactive, user-steerable recommendation in industrial settings.

</details>


### [157] [TRACE: A Multi-Layer Benchmark for Human AI Controller Coordination Under Drift and Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06657)
*Joshua Zuniga,Srinivasan Subramanian,Ramya Madhuri Narapureddy,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于诊断人机协同控制系统中漂移（drift）的基准数据集，基于ALFRED任务生成1,918条跨五层执行层的时间对齐漂移轨迹，并配套去泄漏评估协议，验证了漂移在多个模型家族下可被识别和归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络物理系统和AI辅助系统将人、AI决策模块和自动化控制器耦合在同一控制回路中，可信度取决于整个回路而非单一模型。然而缺乏时间对齐、多层次的基准来捕获漂移和故障在各层之间的传播，导致无法诊断协调失效的位置、原因及恢复方法。

Method: 从ALFRED（日常家务任务的接地指令基准）中提取轨迹，向五层执行层（状态、观察、决策、规则、控制）注入受控漂移，生成1,918条标记轨迹。每条轨迹包含逐步骤的时间对齐记录，标注漂移类型、受影响层、起始时间、责任主体和因果机制，并由独立评分者验证一致性。同时设计了去除近完美起始时间泄漏的"泄漏感知"评估协议，并在经典、循环和注意力三类模型家族上进行基线研究。

Result: 在诚实协议下，漂移识别和归因能力在各类模型中都显著高于随机和多数类基线：受影响层宏F1约0.70，责任主体约0.85，因果机制约0.49。此外，重型注意力模型在此符号基准上并未优于更简单的模型。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了多层次、时间对齐漂移诊断基准的空白，证明了漂移可在跨层轨迹上被可靠识别和归因，但复杂注意力架构在此类符号任务上无明显优势，为后续研究和模型选择提供了参考。

Abstract: Modern cyber-physical and AI-assisted systems couple human operators, AI decision modules, and automated controllers in a single control loop, so trustworthiness depends on the whole loop, not any one model. Yet no standard benchmark captures time-aligned, multi-layer traces of how drift and failures propagate across these layers, so we cannot diagnose where coordination breaks down, why, or how to recover. This paper targets one facet of that gap: drift, a deviation that can originate in any stack layer and that conventional single-modality monitoring cannot localize to a layer or pin to an onset time. We construct a benchmark by injecting controlled drift into traces derived from ALFRED, a grounded-instruction benchmark for everyday household tasks, yielding 1,918 drifted traces. Each trace is a time-aligned sequence of per-step records across five execution layers (state, observation, decision, rules, control), labeled with the drift type, affected layer, onset time, responsible actor, and causal mechanism, and validated by independent raters with inter-annotator agreement reported. We pair the dataset with a leak-aware protocol that removes a near-perfect onset leak, and a baseline study across classical, recurrent, and attention-based model families. Under this honest protocol, drift is identifiable and attributable well above random and majority baselines across every family (affected layer macro-F1 near 0.70, responsible actor near 0.85, causal mechanism near 0.49), and heavy attention offers no advantage over simpler models on this symbolic benchmark.

</details>


### [158] [CellWorld: From Gene-Level Reconstruction to Latent Cell Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06659)
*Haiping Liu,Qian Zhao,Lijing Lin,Jingyuan Sun,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CellWorld，一种用于空间转录组学的基础模型，通过在潜在空间中预测被掩蔽细胞的表征（而非直接重建基因表达值）进行预训练，从而避免了复制技术性变异并提升了表征的可迁移性。在4600万人类细胞上预训练了四种规模变体（5.74M-94.56M参数），实验表明模型容量提升带来性能增长，即使是小模型也在所有基准上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组学基础模型主要重建被掩蔽的基因身份或表达值，这可能鼓励模型复制测定特异性的技术变异，限制了表征的可迁移性。作者旨在通过将预测目标从观测的基因测量转移到潜在细胞表征来规避这一问题。

Method: 引入CellWorld，从可见的空间上下文和有限的部分表达提示中预测被掩蔽细胞的潜在表征，而非直接重建基因表达值。在一个包含4600万人类细胞的数据集上预训练了四种规模的变体（5.74M至94.56M可训练参数），并进行受控的缩放实验。

Result: 性能随模型容量提升而提高，尤其在空间任务上；空间迁移更依赖充分优化和广泛的生物学来源多样性而非仅细胞数量。在四个外部数据集上，CellWorld-Small（5.74M参数）在所有11个线性探针基准和7个微调空间基准上超越所有基线。冻结的CellWorld-Large仅在5%数据上预训练便在所有7个空间基准上超越所有完全微调的基线。

Conclusion: 潜在空间预测预训练为空间转录组学基础模型提供了一条可扩展的道路，能够有效避免复制技术性变异，实现更强的表征可迁移性。

Abstract: This paper shows that latent-space predictive pretraining can provide a scalable route to foundation models for spatial transcriptomics. Existing spatial transcriptomics foundation models primarily reconstruct masked gene identities or expression values, potentially encouraging the reproduction of assay-specific technical variation and limiting representation transferability. To avoid directly reconstructing such variation, we shift the prediction target from observed gene measurements to latent cell representations and introduce CellWorld, which predicts the latent representations of masked cells from visible spatial context and a limited partial-expression hint. We pretrain four CellWorld variants, spanning 5.74M to 94.56M trainable parameters, on a corpus of 46 million human cells. Our controlled scaling experiments show that performance improves with model capacity, particularly on spatial tasks, while spatial transfer depends more on sufficient optimization and broad biological source diversity than on cell count alone. Across four held-out datasets, even CellWorld-Small, with 5.74M trainable parameters, outperforms every baseline on all 11 linear-probe benchmarks and all seven fine-tuned spatial benchmarks. Most notably, a frozen CellWorld-Large pretrained on only 5\% of the corpus with broad biological source coverage outperforms every fully fine-tuned baseline across all seven spatial benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/UoM-HealthAI/CellWorld.

</details>


### [159] [Vehicle routing problem using deep reinforcement learning - A case study about truck planning in the industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06668)
*Siliang Lu,Dan Hu,Lili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三个工业物流案例，探讨了如何将基于深度强化学习（DRL）的车辆路径优化应用于现实中的外部卡车网络设计。结果表明，DRL智能体优化的路线相比基线方案可降低超过10%的总成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆路径问题（VRP）在管理科学和工业界长期受到关注，但现实场景中往往存在诸多复杂约束（如信息不透明、不确定性、非理性行为等），使得传统数学模型在实际部署中面临挑战，需要更智能的优化方法。

Method: 论文针对三个不同的物流用例（外部卡车网络设计），采用基于深度强化学习（DRL）的车辆路径优化方法，并通过工业案例研究验证其可行性。

Result: 深度强化学习智能体优化的路线相比基线结果节省了超过10%的总成本。

Conclusion: DRL在车辆路径优化中展现出显著的实际应用价值；未来研究可将DRL算法推广到更多VRP变体问题中。

Abstract: As an important component of the supply chain industry, transportation has experienced rapid development in the past decade with the assistance of digital platforms and intelligent algorithms. Within the field of transportation research, Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP) has remained a persistent and enduring challenge. In the realm of management science, experts, and scholars from both the industrial and academic sectors have continuously explored optimization models and algorithms to effectively address routing problems, from the classical Traveling Salesman Problem to the more general Vehicle Routing Problem. These models and algorithms are applied in real-world industrial scenarios to achieve cost optimization and reduce carbon footprints. However, due to the complexity of real-world problems, numerous specific constraints are often added, and challenges such as information opacity, uncertainty, and irrational human behavior may arise. Therefore, deploying and optimizing mathematical models for VRP in practical scenarios while maintaining optimal results poses numerous challenges. This paper discusses and provides solutions for three different logistic use cases involving external truck network design. Through these industrial case study, the paper introduces how deep reinforcement learning-based vehicle routing optimization has been implemented. As a result, it can be observed that the routes optimized by reinforcement learning agent have over 10% total cost compared to baseline results. Furthermore, the paper proposes that in future research, DRL algorithms for vehicle routing problems could be generalized into more variations of VRP.

</details>


### [160] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics of Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06694)
*Joohee Choi,Junhyeong Lee,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CGMas是一个多智能体框架，利用大语言模型自动化聚合物粗粒化建模全流程（拓扑构建、平衡、映射、势函数推导与验证），在27个均聚物和共聚物任务中全部完成，22个任务密度误差在5%以内，并将模拟时间从38-88分钟缩短至1分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自下而上的粗粒化建模过程繁琐且劳动密集，因为粗粒化分辨率是设计选择，缺乏可迁移的参数集，每个聚合物映射都需要重新推导势函数，需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出CGMas多智能体框架，包含：LLM推理智能体从聚合物名称推断全原子拓扑结构；分层自校正机制解决不饱和、含杂原子和极性聚合物的物理错误；下游智能体分别负责系统平衡、粗粒化映射、Boltzmann反演推导势函数及与原子参考的基准验证。

Result: CGMas成功完成全部27个均聚物和共聚物任务，其中22个任务的全原子密度匹配误差在5%以内，计算时间从38-88分钟大幅缩短至1分钟。

Conclusion: 该工作证明智能体LLM能够作为自动化聚合物粗粒化的可行路径，显著降低粗粒化建模的门槛并提升效率。

Abstract: Coarse-grained (CG) molecular dynamics extends polymer simulation beyond the scales accessible to all-atom (AA) methods, but bottom-up CG modeling is laborious. The CG resolution is a design choice, so a transferable parameter set is generally not available and the potentials are derived anew for each polymer mapping. Here we present CGMas, a multi-agent framework that automates topology construction, equilibration, mapping, potential derivation, and validation from a natural-language specification of the polymer and target resolution. A large-language-model (LLM) reasoning agent infers the AA topology from polymer name, while layered self-correction resolves physical errors common to unsaturated, heteroatom-containing, and polar polymers. Downstream agents equilibrate the system, map it onto CG representation, derive potentials through Boltzmann inversion, and benchmark the model against its atomistic reference. CGMas completed all 27 homopolymer and copolymer tasks, matched the AA density to within 5% in 22, and reduced simulation from 38-88 min to 1 min, establishing agentic LLMs as a route to automated polymer coarse-graining.

</details>


### [161] [AgentPatch: Coarse-to-Fine Weak-Task Repair for Merging Agentic Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06699)
*Zibo Shao,Baochen Xiong,Chengdong Xu,Linhui Xiao,Kaichen Li,Haoran Gong,Yan Li,Yaguang Song,Xiaoshan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentPatch，一个无需训练的从粗到细修复框架，用于合并Agentic多模态大语言模型，通过弱任务独特残差恢复和智能体引导的行为关键修补，解决非对称能力保留和行为关键遗忘两大挑战，在六个基准上提升合并性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic MLLMs针对特定工具或环境进行专门化设计，难以整合为单一通用模型。模型合并时存在两个关键问题：一是非对称能力保留（不同交互复杂度的能力被不均匀保留，导致弱任务）；二是行为关键遗忘（丢失关键决策行为会破坏长程执行）。

Method: 提出AgentPatch框架：(1)选择稳定的合并骨干模型；(2)通过弱任务独特残差恢复（Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery）恢复被稀释的弱任务特定信号；(3)应用智能体引导的行为关键修补（Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch），在显式能力保护下恢复决定性行为。无需路由或集成，产生单一静态检查点。

Result: 在六个智能体和多模态基准上，AgentPatch显著改进多种合并骨干模型，缓解弱任务退化，并更好地平衡弱任务恢复与互补搜索、智能体视觉处理能力的保持。

Conclusion: AgentPatch能够在不增加推理开销的情况下，通过训练无关的粗到细修复机制有效提升Agentic MLLM合并的质量，为多智能体模型整合提供了一种实用且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend multimodal perception and reasoning with planning, tool use, and interaction in dynamic environments. Yet current models are specialized for particular tools or environments, complicating consolidation into a single generalist. We formulate Agentic MLLM Merging and identify two challenges: asymmetric capability preservation, whereby capabilities with different interaction complexity are retained unevenly, producing weak tasks after merging, and behavior-critical forgetting, whereby losing decisive actions can derail long-horizon execution. We propose AgentPatch, a training-free coarse-to-fine repair framework. It selects a stable merged backbone, restores diluted weak-task-specific signals through Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery, and applies an Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch that recovers decisive behaviors under explicit capability protection. AgentPatch produces a single static checkpoint without routing or ensembles. Experiments across six agentic and multimodal benchmarks show that AgentPatch improves diverse merged backbones, alleviates weak-task degradation, and better balances weak-task recovery with the preservation of complementary search and agentic visual processing capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/ziboshao/AgentPatch.

</details>


### [162] [WebRider: Persona-Conditioned Intent Controllers for Live-Web Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06704)
*Zhi Li,Tao Zhou,Yeqing Li,Eugene Ie,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebRider 提出将网页委托任务从"仅关注最终答案"转向"完整策略约束"(意图契约)的范式。它通过分层架构(控制器-守卫中间层-工具层)将委托策略形式化为可审计的契约,并在4180万(4096)个真实网页契约基准 RiderBench 上验证,同时用守卫中间层训练8B动作策略模型,证明浏览路径应作为一等公民对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时网页代理仅以最终答案评估,忽略了委托任务的策略约束(验证什么、如何处理不确定性、偏好、何时停止)。审计发现强控制器99.2%任务完成但仅38.8%满足全部策略约束,说明"完成≠忠实",亟需将浏览过程本身纳入评估与学习。

Method: 1) 将委托策略形式化为"意图契约"(intent contract):记录目标、约束、证据义务、答案形式、任务局部人设控制;2) 分层架构:顶层控制器维护契约,中层将意图实现为守卫可执行动作(guarded executable actions),工具层通过浏览器/搜索/地图工具执行;3) 构建RiderBench基准:4096个实时网页契约、42个公共网站,同时审计内部契约状态与可见用户体验。

Result: 发现关键差距:强控制器任务完成率99.2%但策略保真率仅38.8%。守卫中间层提供高质量训练信号,8B动作策略模型通过该接口训练,在固定控制器下优于仅可执行基线。证明了可审计、可人工评判、可学习且不混淆动作实现与最终答案决策。

Conclusion: WebRider 表明将浏览路径作为一等公民是必要的:它使系统可审计、可人工评判、可学习,弥补了"完成"与"忠实"之间的鸿沟,为实时网页委托代理提供了新的评估与训练范式。

Abstract: Delegating a web task involves more than asking a question; it requires transferring a policy: what to verify, how to handle uncertainty, which preferences matter, and when to stop. Yet, current live-web agents are evaluated solely on the final answer, ignoring the policy constraints that define the delegation. A plausible final answer can conceal violations of that policy. Our full live audit reveals this critical gap: a strong controller completes 99.2% of tasks but honors all policy constraints in only 38.8% of cases. Finishing does not imply fidelity. WebRider bridges this gap by formalizing the delegated policy as an intent contract---an operational record of goals, constraints, evidence obligations, answer form, and task-local persona controls that must hold even as web pages change. WebRider employs a hierarchical architecture: a top-layer controller maintains the contract, a middle layer realizes intentions as guarded executable actions, and a tool layer executes these actions via browser, search, and maps tools. Our benchmark, RiderBench, evaluates this design on 4,096 live-web contracts across 42 public websites, auditing both the internal contract state and the visible user experience to determine if a rollout preserved its policy and if the steps were persona-consistent. The guarded middle interface also serves as a high-quality training signal; an 8B action-policy model trained through this interface outperforms executable-only baselines under a fixed controller. By making the browsing path a first-class object, WebRider enables a system that is auditable, human-judgeable, and learnable without conflating action realization with final-answer decisions. Dataset URL: hf.co/datasets/WebRider/WebRider.

</details>


### [163] [MolBioKG: Grounding Out-of-Graph Molecules in Biomedical Knowledge Graphs via Multi-Resolution Structural Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06713)
*Yiming Zhang,Hikaru Shindo,Shuan Chen,Kaushalya Madhawa,Jun Jin Choong,Yuna Oikawa,Takashi Fujiwara,Keisuke Ozawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MolBioKG系统，解决生物医学知识图谱中"图外分子"的冷启动问题。该系统采用双层架构，通过多分辨率结构锚定将2.74百万个未注册分子与9.6百万边的知识图谱连接起来，仅需SMILES字符串即可进行检索与推理，无需任务特定训练，在多跳推理和跨图泛化任务上显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准生物医学知识图谱管道假设查询分子已作为图实体存在，导致未注册分子与图谱脱节（即"图外分子问题"）。这一冷启动挑战阻碍了知识图谱在新分子发现中的应用，需要一种无需重新构建或微调即可将任意新分子接入知识图谱的方法。

Method: MolBioKG采用两层系统：(1)构建包含2.74百万分子的索引层（以脚手架、片段、官能团和指纹表示），连接至9.6百万边的知识图谱；(2)通过多分辨率结构锚定将未见分子映射到图实体。推理机制包括两种：静态多锚点检索（使用倒数排名融合RRF）和Adapt-KG（基于工具调用的LLM策略进行自适应遍历）。

Result: 在跨图链接恢复、复杂多跳推理和图外泛化三项任务上，MolBioKG均优于强基线。多跳推理的Hits@10从0.585提升至0.876，图外目标召回率从0.145提升至0.269，且预测结果保持可追溯的结构锚点和来源归属的知识图谱证据。

Conclusion: MolBioKG有效解决了图外分子的冷启动问题，使研究者能够仅凭SMILES字符串将任意分子接入生物医学知识图谱进行推理。其可追溯的结构锚点和来源证据增强了预测的可解释性与可信度，为药物发现中的知识图谱应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) accelerate drug discovery, but standard pipelines assume query molecules already exist as graph entities, leaving unregistered molecules disconnected. We address this cold-start challenge, termed the out-of-graph molecule problem, by introducing MolBioKG. This two-layer system grounds unseen molecules in biomedical evidence via multi-resolution structural anchoring. It connects an index of 2.74 million molecules (represented by scaffolds, fragments, functional groups, and fingerprints) to a 9.6-million-edge KG. Given only a SMILES string, MolBioKG retrieves structurally related graph entities and traverses their biomedical neighborhoods without task-specific training. It features two inference mechanisms: static multi-anchor retrieval using Reciprocal Rank Fusion, and Adapt-KG, a tool-using LLM policy for adaptive traversal. Evaluated across in-graph link recovery, complex multi-hop reasoning, and out-of-graph generalization, MolBioKG outperforms strong baselines. Notably, it raises Hits@10 from 0.585 to 0.876 in multi-hop reasoning and out-of-graph target recall from 0.145 to 0.269, all while ensuring predictions retain traceable structural anchors and source-attributed KG evidence.

</details>


### [164] [The Optimizer Is the Agent: Reasoning-Driven Search across Prompts, Programs, and ML Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06714)
*Junbo Li,Boyi Liu,Canwen Xu,Yite Wang,Yuxiong He,Zhangyang Wang,Qiang Liu,Zhewei Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReASearch是一个统一的推理驱动优化框架，让单个工具使用智能体自主决定评估什么、如何诊断失败、做哪些修改以及何时验证或重启，将传统外部控制器（如进化搜索、bandits等）的搜索策略内化到智能体自身的推理过程中。该框架通过共享的智能体循环和领域特定工具，统一优化提示词、程序和ML工作流，在14个任务上与专用优化系统竞争甚至超越，增益达2%-40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化系统依赖显式的外部循环控制器（如进化搜索、bandits、文本梯度方法）来引导优化过程，作者质疑是否可以将这些搜索策略内化到单一工具使用智能体中，从而简化系统设计并实现跨领域的统一优化。

Method: ReASearch采用统一的智能体循环和领域特定工具，智能体自主进行结果分析、失败诊断、编辑决策和验证/重启判断，并通过持久记忆在长时间跨度内精炼策略。同一流程框架实例化到提示词、程序和ML工作流三个领域。

Result: 在14个多样化任务上，ReASearch与专用优化系统表现相当且多数情况下更优，相比强领域特定基线获得2%-40%的提升，某些情况下发现优于人类已知最佳结果的方案。此外，通常由显式控制器实现的复杂搜索行为从智能体推理过程中自然涌现。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，传统的显式外部搜索控制器可以被单个推理驱动的智能体所替代，复杂的搜索行为能够从智能体的自主推理中自然涌现，为统一的跨领域优化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent systems for optimizing prompts, programs, and ML workflows typically rely on explicit outer-loop controllers such as evolutionary search, bandits, or textual-gradient methods. We ask a fundamentally different question: how much of this search policy can be internalized by a single tool-using agent? We present ReASearch, a unified framework for reasoning-driven optimization in which the agent autonomously decides what to evaluate, how to diagnose failures, which edits to make, and when to verify or restart. Rather than serving only as a proposal generator guided by hand-designed heuristics, the agent actively analyzes outcomes, allocates budget, and refines its strategy over long horizons through persistent memory. With a shared agent loop and domain-specific tools, ReASearch instantiates the exact same scaffold to optimize prompts, programs, and ML workflows. Across 14 diverse tasks, it is competitive with and mostly better than specialized optimization systems, achieving gains of 2% to 40% over strong domain-specific baselines, and in some cases discovering solutions that improve on prior human best-known results. Crucially, we observe that complex search behaviors, which are typically implemented by explicit controllers, emerge naturally from the agent's reasoning process.

</details>
